Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.handleGet(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 533-580
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name.
     *
     * @param t The traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     */
Method code:
533 |     void handleGet(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent, String name) {
534 |       if (maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(t, n, parent, name)) return;
535 | 
536 |       Ref.Type type = Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET;
537 |       if (parent != null) {
538 |         switch (parent.getType()) {
539 |           case Token.IF:
540 |           case Token.TYPEOF:
541 |           case Token.VOID:
542 |           case Token.NOT:
543 |           case Token.BITNOT:
544 |           case Token.POS:
545 |           case Token.NEG:
546 |             break;
547 |           case Token.CALL:
548 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
549 |                    ? Ref.Type.CALL_GET
550 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
551 |             break;
552 |           case Token.NEW:
553 |             type = n == parent.getFirstChild()
554 |                    ? Ref.Type.DIRECT_GET
555 |                    : Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
556 |             break;
557 |           case Token.OR:
558 |           case Token.AND:
559 |             // This node is x or y in (x||y) or (x&&y). We only know that an
560 |             // alias is not getting created for this name if the result is used
561 |             // in a boolean context or assigned to the same name
562 |             // (e.g. var a = a || {}).
563 |             type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
564 |             break;
565 |           case Token.HOOK:
566 |             if (n != parent.getFirstChild()) {
567 |               // This node is y or z in (x?y:z). We only know that an alias is
568 |               // not getting created for this name if the result is assigned to
569 |               // the same name (e.g. var a = a ? a : {}).
570 |               type = determineGetTypeForHookOrBooleanExpr(t, parent, name);
571 |             }
572 |             break;
573 |           default:
574 |             type = Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET;
575 |             break;
576 |         }
577 |       }
578 | 
579 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, name, type);
580 |     }

/**
   * Collapses definitions of the collapsible properties of a global name.
   * Recurses on subnames that also represent JavaScript objects with
   * collapsible properties.
   *
   * @param n A node representing a global name
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n}
   */
Method code:
441 |   private void collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(Name n, String alias) {
442 |     boolean canCollapseChildNames = n.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames();
443 | 
444 |     // Handle this name first so that nested object literals get unrolled.
445 |     if (n.canCollapse() && canCollapseChildNames) {
446 |       updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(n, alias);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     if (n.props != null) {
450 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
451 |         // Recurse first so that saved node ancestries are intact when needed.
452 |         collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(
453 |             p, appendPropForAlias(alias, p.name));
454 | 
455 |         if (!p.inExterns && canCollapseChildNames && p.declaration != null &&
456 |             p.declaration.node != null &&
457 |             p.declaration.node.getParent() != null &&
458 |             p.declaration.node.getParent().getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
459 |           updateSimpleDeclaration(
460 |               appendPropForAlias(alias, p.name), p, p.declaration);
461 |         }
462 |       }
463 |     }
464 |   }

===== M0018 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(Name,String,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 815-842
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds global variable "stubs" for any properties of a global name that are
   * only set in a local scope or read but never set.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name of the object whose properties we are
   *     adding stubs for (e.g. "a$b$c")
   * @param parent The node to which new global variables should be added
   *     as children
   * @param addAfter The child of after which new
   *     variables should be added (may be null)
   * @return The number of variables added
   */
Method code:
815 |   private int addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(
816 |       Name n, String alias, Node parent, Node addAfter) {
817 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent));
818 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(addAfter);
819 |     int numStubs = 0;
820 |     if (n.props != null) {
821 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
822 |         if (p.needsToBeStubbed()) {
823 |           String propAlias = appendPropForAlias(alias, p.name);
824 |           Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
825 |           Node newVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode)
826 |               .copyInformationFromForTree(addAfter);
827 |           parent.addChildAfter(newVar, addAfter);
828 |           addAfter = newVar;
829 |           numStubs++;
830 |           compiler.reportCodeChange();
831 | 
832 |           // Determine if this is a constant var by checking the first
833 |           // reference to it. Don't check the declaration, as it might be null.
834 |           if (p.refs.get(0).node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(
835 |                 Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
836 |             nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
837 |           }
838 |         }
839 |       }
840 |     }
841 |     return numStubs;
842 |   }

===== M0020 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 204-228
Documentation:

Method code:
204 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
205 |     JSDocInfo jsDocInfo = handleJsDoc(node);
206 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
207 |     if (jsDocInfo != null) {
208 |       irNode.setJSDocInfo(jsDocInfo);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
212 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
213 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
214 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
215 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
216 |     } else {
217 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
218 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
219 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
220 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
221 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
222 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
223 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
224 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
225 |       }
226 |     }
227 |     return irNode;
228 |   }

Method code:
311 |     @Override
312 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
313 |       switch (n.getType()) {
314 |         case Token.WHILE:
315 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
316 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
317 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
318 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
319 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
320 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
321 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
322 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
323 |           }
324 |           break;
325 | 
326 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
327 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
328 |           break;
329 | 
330 |         case Token.NAME:
331 |         case Token.STRING:
332 |           annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
333 |           break;
334 |       }
335 |     }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1889-1899
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
1889 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
1890 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
1891 |     int len = s.length();
1892 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
1893 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
1894 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
1895 |         return false;
1896 |       }
1897 |     }
1898 |     return true;
1899 |   }

===== M0053 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getNameForObjLitKey(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 341-394
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the fully qualified name corresponding to an object literal key,
     * as long as it and its prefix property names are valid JavaScript
     * identifiers. The object literal may be nested inside of other object
     * literals.
     *
     * For example, if called with node {@code n} representing "z" in any of
     * the following expressions, the result would be "w.x.y.z":
     * <code> var w = {x: {y: {z: 0}}}; </code>
     * <code> w.x = {y: {z: 0}}; </code>
     * <code> w.x.y = {'a': 0, 'z': 0}; </code>
     *
     * @param n A child of an OBJLIT node
     * @return The global name, or null if {@code n} doesn't correspond to the
     *   key of an object literal that can be named
     */
Method code:
341 |     String getNameForObjLitKey(Node n) {
342 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
343 |       Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
344 | 
345 |       Node gramps = parent.getParent();
346 |       if (gramps == null) {
347 |         return null;
348 |       }
349 | 
350 |       Node greatGramps = gramps.getParent();
351 |       String name;
352 |       switch (gramps.getType()) {
353 |         case Token.NAME:
354 |           // VAR
355 |           //   NAME (gramps)
356 |           //     OBJLIT (parent)
357 |           //       STRING (n)
358 |           if (greatGramps == null ||
359 |               greatGramps.getType() != Token.VAR) {
360 |             return null;
361 |           }
362 |           name = gramps.getString();
363 |           break;
364 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
365 |           // ASSIGN (gramps)
366 |           //   NAME|GETPROP
367 |           //   OBJLIT (parent)
368 |           //     STRING (n)
369 |           Node lvalue = gramps.getFirstChild();
370 |           name = lvalue.getQualifiedName();
371 |           break;
372 |         case Token.STRING:
373 |           // OBJLIT
374 |           //   STRING (gramps)
375 |           //     OBJLIT (parent)
376 |           //       STRING (n)
377 |           if (greatGramps != null &&
378 |               greatGramps.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
379 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(gramps);
380 |           } else {
381 |             return null;
382 |           }
383 |           break;
384 |         default:
385 |           return null;
386 |       }
387 |       if (name != null) {
388 |         String key = n.getString();
389 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key)) {
390 |           return name + '.' + key;
391 |         }
392 |       }
393 |       return null;
394 |     }

243 |     int N = stringBufferTop;

246 |         System.arraycopy(stringBuffer, 0, tmp, 0, N);

251 |   }

185 |   @Override
186 |   public int hashCode() {
187 |     int hashCode = 0;

190 |     }

201 |         param = param.getNext();
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |     return hashCode;
205 |   }

   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.

Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {

302 |     }

===== M0073 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 83-113
Documentation:

Method code:
83 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
84 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
85 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
86 | 
87 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
88 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
89 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
90 | 
91 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
92 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
93 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
94 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
95 |         declareVar(fnName, fnNameNode, n, null, null, n);
96 |       }
97 | 
98 |       // Args: Declare function variables
99 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
100 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
101 |            a = a.getNext()) {
102 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
103 |         declareVar(a.getString(), a, args, n, null, n);
104 |       }
105 | 
106 |       // Body
107 |       scanVars(body, n);
108 |     } else {
109 |       // It's the global block
110 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
111 |       scanVars(n, null);
112 |     }
113 |   }

1061 |       throws IOException {

===== M0086 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.checkNamespaces()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 242-261
Documentation:
/**
   * Runs through all namespaces (prefixes of classes and enums), and checks if
   * any of them have been used in an unsafe way.
   */
Method code:
242 |   private void checkNamespaces() {
243 |     for (Name name : nameMap.values()) {
244 |       if (name.isNamespace() && name.refs != null &&
245 |           (name.aliasingGets > 0 || name.localSets + name.globalSets > 1)) {
246 |         boolean initialized = name.declaration != null;
247 |         for (Ref ref : name.refs) {
248 |           if (ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_GLOBAL ||
249 |               ref.type == Ref.Type.SET_FROM_LOCAL) {
250 |             if (initialized) {
251 |               warnAboutNamespaceRedefinition(name, ref);
252 |             }
253 | 
254 |             initialized = true;
255 |           } else if (ref.type == Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET) {
256 |             warnAboutNamespaceAliasing(name, ref);
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 |     }
261 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 115-141
Documentation:

Method code:
115 |   @Override
116 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
117 |     GlobalNamespace namespace;
118 |     if (collapsePropertiesOnExternTypes) {
119 |       namespace = new GlobalNamespace(compiler, externs, root);
120 |     } else {
121 |       namespace = new GlobalNamespace(compiler, root);
122 |     }
123 | 
124 |     if (inlineAliases) {
125 |       inlineAliases(namespace);
126 |     }
127 |     nameMap = namespace.getNameIndex();
128 |     globalNames = namespace.getNameForest();
129 |     checkNamespaces();
130 | 
131 |     for (Name n : globalNames) {
132 |       flattenReferencesToCollapsibleDescendantNames(n, n.name);
133 |     }
134 | 
135 |     // We collapse property definitions after collapsing property references
136 |     // because this step can alter the parse tree above property references,
137 |     // invalidating the node ancestry stored with each reference.
138 |     for (Name n : globalNames) {
139 |       collapseDeclarationOfNameAndDescendants(n, n.name);
140 |     }
141 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 502-552
Documentation:

Method code:
502 |    @Override
503 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
504 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
505 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
506 |       if (name == null) {
507 |         name = new Name();
508 |         name.setIdentifier("");
509 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
510 |       }
511 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
512 |       Node newName = transform(name);
513 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
514 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
515 |         // declaration.
516 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
517 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
518 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
519 |         // same line as the function keyword.
520 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
521 |             functionNode.getLp();
522 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
523 |       }
524 | 
525 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
526 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
527 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
528 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
529 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
530 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
531 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
532 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
533 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
534 |       if (fnName != null) {
535 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
536 |       } else {
537 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
538 |       }
539 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
540 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
541 | 
542 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
543 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
544 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
545 |       }
546 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
547 | 
548 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
549 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
550 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
551 |      return node;
552 |     }

===== M0094 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/TokenStream.java
Original range: 294-319
Documentation:

Method code:
294 |     public static boolean isJSIdentifier(String s) {
295 |         int length = s.length();
296 | 
297 |         if (length == 0 || !Character.isJavaIdentifierStart(s.charAt(0)))
298 |             return false;
299 | 
300 |         for (int i=1; i<length; i++) {
301 |             char c = s.charAt(i);
302 |             if (!Character.isJavaIdentifierPart(c)) {
303 |                 if (c == '\\') {
304 |                     if (! ((i + 5) < length)
305 |                         && (s.charAt(i + 1) == 'u')
306 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 2), 0)
307 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 3), 0)
308 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 4), 0)
309 |                         && 0 <= Kit.xDigitToInt(s.charAt(i + 5), 0)) {
310 |                         return true;
311 |                      }
312 |                 }
313 | 
314 |                 return false;
315 |             }
316 |         }
317 | 
318 |         return true;
319 |     }

===== M0106 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.flattenReferencesTo(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 316-342
Documentation:
/**
   * Flattens all references to a collapsible property of a global name except
   * its initial definition.
   *
   * @param n A global property name (e.g. "a.b" or "a.b.c.d")
   * @param alias The flattened name (e.g. "a$b" or "a$b$c$d")
   */
Method code:
316 |   private void flattenReferencesTo(Name n, String alias) {
317 |     if (n.refs != null) {
318 |       String originalName = n.fullName();
319 |       for (Ref r : n.refs) {
320 |         Node rParent = r.node.getParent();
321 | 
322 |         // There are two cases when we shouldn't flatten a reference:
323 |         // 1) Object literal keys, because duplicate keys show up as refs.
324 |         // 2) References inside a complex assign. (a = x.y = 0). These are
325 |         //    called TWIN references, because they show up twice in the
326 |         //    reference list. Only collapse the set, not the alias.
327 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(r.node, rParent) &&
328 |             (r.getTwin() == null || r.isSet())) {
329 |           flattenNameRef(alias, r.node, rParent, originalName);
330 |         }
331 |       }
332 |     }
333 | 
334 |     // Flatten all occurrences of a name as a prefix of its subnames. For
335 |     // example, if {@code n} corresponds to the name "a.b", then "a.b" will be
336 |     // replaced with "a$b" in all occurrences of "a.b.c", "a.b.c.d", etc.
337 |     if (n.props != null) {
338 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
339 |         flattenPrefixes(alias, p, 1);
340 |       }
341 |     }
342 |   }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 587-613
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
587 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
588 |     ObjectType owner = null;
589 | 
590 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
591 |     // to discrete object types.
592 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
593 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
594 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
595 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
596 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
597 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
598 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     if (owner == null) {
603 |       return;
604 |     }
605 | 
606 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
607 |     if (typeSet == null) {
608 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
609 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
610 |     }
611 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
612 |     typeSet.add(owner);
613 |   }

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null
191 |         || parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION
192 |         || n == parent.getFirstChild()) {
193 |       if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
194 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(n.getString());
195 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
196 |         Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
197 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(nameNode.getString());
198 |       }
199 | 
200 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }
204 |   }

===== M0130 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.flattenPrefixes(String,Name,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 353-378
Documentation:
/**
   * Flattens all occurrences of a name as a prefix of subnames beginning
   * with a particular subname.
   *
   * @param n A global property name (e.g. "a.b.c.d")
   * @param alias A flattened prefix name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param depth The difference in depth between the property name and
   *    the prefix name (e.g. 2)
   */
Method code:
353 |   private void flattenPrefixes(String alias, Name n, int depth) {
354 |     // Only flatten the prefix of a name declaration if the name being
355 |     // initialized is fully qualified (i.e. not an object literal key).
356 |     String originalName = n.fullName();
357 |     if (n.declaration != null && n.declaration.node != null &&
358 |         n.declaration.node.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
359 |       flattenNameRefAtDepth(alias, n.declaration.node, depth, originalName);
360 |     }
361 | 
362 |     if (n.refs != null) {
363 |       for (Ref r : n.refs) {
364 | 
365 |         // References inside a complex assign (a = x.y = 0)
366 |         // have twins. We should only flatten one of the twins.
367 |         if (r.getTwin() == null || r.isSet()) {
368 |           flattenNameRefAtDepth(alias, r.node, depth, originalName);
369 |         }
370 |       }
371 |     }
372 | 
373 |     if (n.props != null) {
374 |       for (Name p : n.props) {
375 |         flattenPrefixes(alias, p, depth + 1);
376 |       }
377 |     }
378 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PrepareAst.PrepareAnnotations.annotateCalls(Node)

Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }
191 |     }

// Gets all the property types, in sorted order.

847 | 
848 |     int[] keys = new int[count];
849 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.next) {
850 |       count--;
851 |       keys[count] = x.type;
852 |     }
853 | 
854 |     Arrays.sort(keys);
855 |     return keys;
856 |   }

1284 |     Node parent = n.getParent();

1286 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 420-446
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
420 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
421 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
422 | 
423 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
424 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
425 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
426 |       return 0;
427 |     }
428 | 
429 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
430 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
431 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
432 |       // a new statistic may have been added
433 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
434 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
435 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
436 |       }
437 |     }
438 | 
439 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
440 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
441 |       return 0;
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
445 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
446 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 361-376
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
361 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
362 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
363 |       Set<String> directives = null;
364 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
365 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
366 |         if (directives == null) {
367 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
368 |         } else {
369 |           directives.add(directive);
370 |         }
371 |       }
372 | 
373 |       if (directives != null) {
374 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
375 |       }
376 |     }

/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array

1115 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {

   */
Method code:
775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
776 |     if (item == null) {

787 |       }
788 |     }

===== M0170 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

118 |         new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange));
119 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
120 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
121 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
122 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
123 |     }
124 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
125 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
126 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
127 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
128 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
129 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
130 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
131 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
132 |     //   function f() {
133 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
134 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
135 |     //   }
136 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
137 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
138 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
139 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
140 |         .process(externs, root);
141 | 
142 |     compiler.setNormalized();
143 |   }

===== M0173 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0183 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 469-497
Documentation:
/**
     * Determines whether a set operation is a constructor or enumeration
     * declaration. The set operation may either be an assignment to a name,
     * a variable declaration, or an object literal key mapping.
     *
     * @param n The node that represents the name being set
     * @param parent Parent node of {@code n} (an ASSIGN, VAR, or OBJLIT node)
     * @return Whether the set operation is either a constructor or enum
     *     declaration
     */
Method code:
469 |     private boolean isConstructorOrEnumDeclaration(Node n, Node parent) {
470 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): This does not handle named constructors
471 |       // function a() {}
472 |       // For legacy reasons, we should not fix this, because we do not
473 |       // know who's depending on the current behavior.
474 | 
475 |       JSDocInfo info;
476 |       int valueNodeType;
477 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
478 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
479 |           info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
480 |           valueNodeType = n.getNext().getType();
481 |           break;
482 |         case Token.VAR:
483 |           info = n.getJSDocInfo();
484 |           if (info == null) {
485 |             info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
486 |           }
487 |           Node valueNode = n.getFirstChild();
488 |           valueNodeType = valueNode != null ? valueNode.getType() : Token.VOID;
489 |           break;
490 |         default:
491 |           return false;
492 |       }
493 |       // Heed the annotations only if they're sensibly used.
494 |       return info != null &&
495 |              (info.isConstructor() && valueNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
496 |               info.hasEnumParameterType() && valueNodeType == Token.OBJECTLIT);
497 |     }

/**
   * Returns true if a name node represents a constant variable.

   * <p>Determining whether a variable is constant has three steps:
   * <ol>
   * <li>In CodingConventionAnnotator, any name that matches the
   *     {@link CodingConvention#isConstant(String)} is annotated with an

   * </ol>

===== M0191 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

===== M0213 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1472-1474
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1472 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1473 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1474 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()

/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *

137 |   boolean parse() {
138 |     int lineno;
139 |     int charno;
140 | 
141 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
142 |     JSTypeExpression type;
143 | 
144 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
145 |     skipEOLs();
146 | 
147 |     JsDocToken token = next();
148 | 
149 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
150 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
151 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
152 |       token = blockInfo.token;
153 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
154 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
155 |       }
156 |     } else {
157 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
158 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
159 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
160 |         // what it was.
161 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
162 |       }
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
166 |     retry: for (;;) {
167 |       switch (token) {
168 |         case ANNOTATION:
169 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
170 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
171 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
172 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
173 | 
174 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
175 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
176 |             if (annotation == null) {
177 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
178 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
179 |             } else {
180 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
181 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
182 | 
183 |               switch (annotation) {
184 |                 case AUTHOR:
185 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
186 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
187 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
188 | 
189 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
190 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
191 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
192 |                     } else {
193 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
194 |                     }
195 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
196 |                   } else {
197 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
198 |                   }
199 |                   continue retry;
200 | 
201 |                 case CONSTANT:
202 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
203 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
204 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
205 |                   }
206 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
207 |                   continue retry;
208 | 
209 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
210 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
211 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
212 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
213 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
214 |                     } else {
215 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
216 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
217 |                     }
218 |                   }
219 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
220 |                   continue retry;
221 | 
222 |                 case DEPRECATED:
223 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
224 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
225 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
226 |                   }
227 | 
228 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
229 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
230 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
231 | 
232 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
233 | 
234 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
235 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
236 |                   }
237 | 
238 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
239 |                   continue retry;
240 | 
241 |                 case INTERFACE:
242 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
243 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
244 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
245 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
246 |                     } else {
247 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
248 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
249 |                     }
250 |                   }
251 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
252 |                   continue retry;
253 | 
254 |                 case DESC:
255 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
256 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
257 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
259 |                     continue retry;
260 |                   } else {
261 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
262 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
263 | 
264 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;

267 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
268 |                     continue retry;
269 |                   }
270 | 
271 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
272 |                   String fileOverview = "";
273 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
274 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
275 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
276 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);

286 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
287 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
288 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
289 |                   }
290 |                   continue retry;

292 |                 case LICENSE:
293 |                 case PRESERVE:

295 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
296 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);

302 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
303 |                     }
304 |                   }

306 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
307 |                   continue retry;

310 |                   token = next();
311 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
312 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();

315 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
316 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(

321 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
322 |                   }

324 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
325 |                   }

354 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
355 |                   skipEOLs();
356 |                   token = next();
357 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
358 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();

380 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {

396 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
397 |                       }

405 |                   }

407 |                   continue retry;

411 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
412 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
413 |                   }

415 |                   continue retry;
416 | 
417 |                 case LENDS:
418 |                   skipEOLs();

448 |                   token = meaningInfo.token;

452 |                   }

459 |                   }

467 |                   }

482 | 
483 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
484 |                 case OVERRIDE:
485 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {

488 |                   }

500 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
501 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));

503 |                     if (type == null) {
504 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
505 |                       // recovering parsing
506 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
507 |                       continue retry;
508 |                     }
509 |                   }
510 | 
511 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
512 |                   token = current();

514 |                   // Save the throw type.
515 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);

518 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
519 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =

525 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
526 |                     }

529 |                   } else {

535 |                   skipEOLs();
536 |                   token = next();
537 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
538 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
539 |                   type = null;
540 | 
541 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
542 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
543 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
544 | 
545 |                     if (type == null) {
546 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
547 |                       // recovering parsing
548 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
549 |                       continue retry;
550 |                     }
551 |                     skipEOLs();
552 |                     token = next();
553 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
554 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
555 |                   }
556 | 
557 |                   String name = null;
558 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
559 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
560 |                     token = next();
561 |                   }

563 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
564 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
565 |                         lineno, charno);
566 |                   } else {
567 |                     name = stream.getString();
568 | 
569 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
570 |                       token = next();
571 | 
572 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
573 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
574 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
575 |                         token = next();
576 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
577 |                           token = next();
578 |                         }
579 |                       }
580 | 
581 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
582 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
583 |                       } else if (type != null) {
584 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
585 |                         // already.
586 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
587 |                       }
588 |                     }
589 | 
590 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
591 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
592 |                     // for handling properties of params.
593 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
594 |                       name = null;
595 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
596 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
597 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
598 |                             lineno, charno);
599 |                       } else {
600 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
601 |                             lineno, charno);
602 |                       }
603 |                     }
604 |                   }
605 | 
606 |                   if (name == null) {
607 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
608 |                     continue retry;
609 |                   }
610 | 
611 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
612 | 
613 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
614 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
615 |                     ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
616 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
617 | 
618 |                     String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
619 | 
620 |                     if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
621 |                       jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
622 |                           paramDescription);
623 |                     }
624 | 
625 |                     token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
626 |                   } else {
627 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
628 |                   }
629 |                   continue retry;
630 | 
631 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
632 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
633 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
634 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
635 |                   }
636 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
637 |                   continue retry;
638 | 
639 |                 case PRIVATE:
640 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
641 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
642 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
643 |                   }
644 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
645 |                   continue retry;
646 | 
647 |                 case PROTECTED:
648 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
649 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
650 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
651 |                   }
652 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
653 |                   continue retry;
654 | 
655 |                 case PUBLIC:
656 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
657 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
658 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
659 |                   }
660 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
661 |                   continue retry;
662 | 
663 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
664 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
665 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
666 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
667 |                   }
668 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
669 |                   continue retry;
670 | 
671 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
672 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
673 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
674 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
675 |                   }
676 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
677 |                   continue retry;
678 | 
679 |                 case MODIFIES:
680 |                   token = parseModifiesTag(next());
681 |                   continue retry;
682 | 
683 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
684 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
685 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
686 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
687 |                   }
688 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
689 |                   continue retry;
690 | 
691 |                 case SEE:
692 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
693 |                     ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
694 |                     String reference = referenceInfo.string;
695 | 
696 |                     if (reference.length() == 0) {
697 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
698 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
699 |                     } else {
700 |                       jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
701 |                     }
702 | 
703 |                     token = referenceInfo.token;
704 |                   } else {
705 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
706 |                   }
707 |                   continue retry;
708 | 
709 |                 case SUPPRESS:
710 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
711 |                   continue retry;
712 | 
713 |                 case TEMPLATE:
714 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
715 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
716 | 
717 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
718 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
719 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
720 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
721 |                       templateTypeName)) {
722 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
723 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
724 |                   }
725 | 
726 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
727 |                   continue retry;
728 | 
729 |                 case VERSION:
730 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
731 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
732 | 
733 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
734 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
735 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
736 |                   } else {
737 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
738 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
739 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
740 |                     }
741 |                   }
742 | 
743 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
744 |                   continue retry;
745 | 
746 |                 case DEFINE:
747 |                 case RETURN:
748 |                 case THIS:
749 |                 case TYPE:
750 |                 case TYPEDEF:
751 |                   skipEOLs();
752 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
753 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
754 | 
755 |                   token = next();
756 | 
757 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
758 | 
759 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
760 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
761 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
762 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
763 |                     }
764 |                   }
765 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
766 | 
767 |                   if (type == null) {
768 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
769 |                     // recovering parsing
770 |                   } else {
771 |                     switch (annotation) {
772 |                       case DEFINE:
773 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
774 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
775 |                               lineno, charno);
776 |                         }
777 |                         break;
778 | 
779 |                       case RETURN:
780 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
781 |                           parser.addWarning(
782 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
783 |                           break;
784 |                         }
785 | 
786 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
787 |                         token = current();
788 | 
789 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
790 |                         if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
791 |                           ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
792 |                               extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
793 | 
794 |                           String returnDescription =
795 |                               returnDescriptionInfo.string;
796 | 
797 |                           if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
798 |                             jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
799 |                                 returnDescription);
800 |                           }
801 | 
802 |                           token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
803 |                         } else {
804 |                           token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
805 |                         }
806 |                         continue retry;
807 | 
808 |                       case THIS:
809 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
810 |                           parser.addWarning(
811 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
812 |                         }
813 |                         break;
814 | 
815 |                       case TYPE:
816 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
817 |                           parser.addWarning(
818 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
819 |                         }
820 |                         break;
821 | 
822 |                       case TYPEDEF:
823 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
824 |                           parser.addWarning(
825 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
826 |                         }
827 |                         break;
828 |                     }
829 | 
830 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
831 |                   }
832 |                   continue retry;
833 |               }
834 |             }
835 |           }
836 |           break;
837 | 
838 |         case EOC:
839 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
840 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
841 |           }
842 | 
843 |           return true;
844 | 
845 |         case EOF:
846 |           // discard any accumulated information
847 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
848 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
849 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
850 |           return false;
851 | 
852 |         case EOL:
853 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
854 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
855 |           }
856 |           token = next();
857 |           continue retry;
858 | 
859 |         default:
860 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
861 |             token = next();
862 |             continue retry;
863 |           } else {
864 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
865 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
866 |             continue retry;
867 |           }
868 |       }
869 | 
870 |       // next token
871 |       token = next();
872 |     }
873 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.

Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {

313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0

===== M0264 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 118-174
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
118 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
119 |     switch (n.getType()) {
120 |       case Token.VAR:
121 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
122 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
123 |              child != null;) {
124 |           Node next = child.getNext();
125 |           Preconditions.checkState(child.getType() == Token.NAME);
126 | 
127 |           String name = child.getString();
128 |           declareVar(name, child, n, parent, null, n);
129 |           child = next;
130 |         }
131 |         return;
132 | 
133 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
134 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
135 |           return;
136 |         }
137 | 
138 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
139 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
140 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
141 |           return;
142 |         }
143 |         declareVar(fnName, n.getFirstChild(), n, parent, null, n);
144 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
145 | 
146 |       case Token.CATCH:
147 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
149 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
150 |         // is the code block
151 | 
152 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
153 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
154 | 
155 |         declareVar(var.getString(), var, n, parent, null, n);
156 |         scanVars(block, n);
157 |         return;  // only one child to scan
158 | 
159 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
160 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
161 |         break;
162 |     }
163 | 
164 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
165 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
166 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
167 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
168 |            child != null;) {
169 |         Node next = child.getNext();
170 |         scanVars(child, n);
171 |         child = next;
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |   }

===== M0271 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 962-982
Documentation:

Method code:
962 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
963 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
964 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
965 |       return s;
966 |     }
967 | 
968 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
969 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
970 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
971 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
972 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
973 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
974 |       // crazy.
975 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
976 |         sb.append(c);
977 |       } else {
978 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
979 |       }
980 |     }
981 |     return sb.toString();
982 |   }

===== M0307 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 574-628
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name
   * that occurs at an ASSIGN node. See comment for
   * {@link #updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration}.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n} (e.g. "a", "a$b$c")
   */
Method code:
574 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(
575 |       Name n, String alias) {
576 |     // NOTE: It's important that we don't add additional nodes
577 |     // (e.g. a var node before the exprstmt) because the exprstmt might be
578 |     // the child of an if statement that's not inside a block).
579 | 
580 |     Ref ref = n.declaration;
581 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getNext();
582 |     Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR);
583 |     Node varParent = ref.node.getAncestor(3);
584 |     Node gramps = ref.node.getAncestor(2);
585 |     boolean isObjLit = rvalue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT;
586 | 
587 |     if (isObjLit && n.canEliminate()) {
588 |       // Eliminate the object literal altogether.
589 |       varParent.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
590 |       ref.node = null;
591 | 
592 |     } else {
593 |       if (rvalue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
594 |         checkForHosedThisReferences(rvalue, n.docInfo, n);
595 |       }
596 | 
597 |       ref.node.getParent().removeChild(rvalue);
598 | 
599 |       Node nameNode = NodeUtil.newName(
600 |           compiler.getCodingConvention(),
601 |           alias, ref.node.getAncestor(2), n.fullName());
602 | 
603 |       if (ref.node.getLastChild().getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
604 |         nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
605 |       }
606 | 
607 |       varNode.addChildToBack(nameNode);
608 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(rvalue);
609 |       varParent.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
610 | 
611 |       // Update the node ancestry stored in the reference.
612 |       ref.node = nameNode;
613 |     }
614 | 
615 |     if (isObjLit) {
616 |       declareVarsForObjLitValues(
617 |           n, alias, rvalue,
618 |           varNode, varParent.getChildBefore(varNode), varParent);
619 |     }
620 | 
621 |     addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(n, alias, varParent, varNode);
622 | 
623 |     if (!varNode.hasChildren()) {
624 |       varParent.removeChild(varNode);
625 |     }
626 | 
627 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
628 |   }

/**
   * Flattens all references to collapsible properties of a global name except
   * their initial definitions. Recurses on subnames.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n}
   */
Method code:
294 |   private void flattenReferencesToCollapsibleDescendantNames(
295 |       Name n, String alias) {
296 |     if (n.props == null) return;
297 | 
298 |     for (Name p : n.props) {

306 |     }

1198 |   static boolean isControlStructure(Node n) {
