===== M0333 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1028-1165
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1028 |   Node parseInputs() {
1029 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1030 | 
1031 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1032 |     // individual file parse trees.
1033 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1034 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1035 |     }
1036 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1037 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1038 |     }
1039 | 
1040 |     // Parse main js sources.
1041 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1042 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1043 | 
1044 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1045 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1046 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1047 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1048 |     }
1049 | 
1050 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1051 | 
1052 |     try {
1053 |       // Parse externs sources.
1054 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1055 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1056 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1057 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1058 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1059 |           return null;
1060 |         }
1061 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1062 |       }
1063 | 
1064 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1065 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1066 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1067 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1068 | 
1069 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1070 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1071 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1072 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1073 |           }
1074 |         }
1075 | 
1076 |         try {
1077 |           inputs =
1078 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1079 |               .manageDependencies(
1080 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1081 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1082 |           report(JSError.make(
1083 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1084 |           return null;
1085 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1086 |           report(JSError.make(
1087 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1088 |           return null;
1089 |         }
1090 |       }
1091 | 
1092 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1093 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1094 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1095 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1096 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1097 |           return null;
1098 |         }
1099 | 
1100 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1101 |         if (n == null) {
1102 |           continue;
1103 |         }
1104 | 
1105 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1106 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1107 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1108 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1109 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1110 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1111 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1112 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1113 | 
1114 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1115 | 
1116 |             externs.add(input);
1117 |             staleInputs = true;
1118 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1119 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1120 |             staleInputs = true;
1121 |           }
1122 |         }
1123 |       }
1124 | 
1125 |       if (staleInputs) {
1126 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1127 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1128 |       }
1129 | 
1130 |       // Build the AST.
1131 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1132 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1133 |         if (n == null) {
1134 |           continue;
1135 |         }
1136 | 
1137 |         if (devMode) {
1138 |           runSanityCheck();
1139 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1140 |             return null;
1141 |           }
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1145 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1146 | 
1147 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1148 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1149 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1150 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1151 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1152 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1153 |         }
1154 | 
1155 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1156 |       }
1157 | 
1158 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1159 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1160 | 
1161 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1162 |     } finally {
1163 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1164 |     }
1165 |   }

235 |                     else if (c=='f') { X="final";id=Id_final; }
236 |                     break L;
237 |                 case 'o': c=s.charAt(0);
238 |                     if (c=='f') { X="float";id=Id_float; }
239 |                     else if (c=='s') { X="short";id=Id_short; }
240 |                     break L;
241 |                 case 'p': X="super";id=Id_super; break L;
242 |                 case 'r': X="throw";id=Id_throw; break L;
243 |                 case 't': X="catch";id=Id_catch; break L;
244 |                 } break L;
245 |             case 6: switch (s.charAt(1)) {
246 |                 case 'a': X="native";id=Id_native; break L;
247 |                 case 'e': c=s.charAt(0);
248 |                     if (c=='d') { X="delete";id=Id_delete; }
249 |                     else if (c=='r') { X="return";id=Id_return; }
250 |                     break L;
251 |                 case 'h': X="throws";id=Id_throws; break L;
252 |                 case 'm': X="import";id=Id_import; break L;
253 |                 case 'o': X="double";id=Id_double; break L;
254 |                 case 't': X="static";id=Id_static; break L;
255 |                 case 'u': X="public";id=Id_public; break L;
256 |                 case 'w': X="switch";id=Id_switch; break L;
257 |                 case 'x': X="export";id=Id_export; break L;
258 |                 case 'y': X="typeof";id=Id_typeof; break L;
259 |                 } break L;
260 |             case 7: switch (s.charAt(1)) {
261 |                 case 'a': X="package";id=Id_package; break L;
262 |                 case 'e': X="default";id=Id_default; break L;
263 |                 case 'i': X="finally";id=Id_finally; break L;
264 |                 case 'o': X="boolean";id=Id_boolean; break L;
265 |                 case 'r': X="private";id=Id_private; break L;
266 |                 case 'x': X="extends";id=Id_extends; break L;
267 |                 } break L;
268 |             case 8: switch (s.charAt(0)) {
269 |                 case 'a': X="abstract";id=Id_abstract; break L;
270 |                 case 'c': X="continue";id=Id_continue; break L;
271 |                 case 'd': X="debugger";id=Id_debugger; break L;
272 |                 case 'f': X="function";id=Id_function; break L;
273 |                 case 'v': X="volatile";id=Id_volatile; break L;
274 |                 } break L;
275 |             case 9: c=s.charAt(0);
276 |                 if (c=='i') { X="interface";id=Id_interface; }
277 |                 else if (c=='p') { X="protected";id=Id_protected; }
278 |                 else if (c=='t') { X="transient";id=Id_transient; }
279 |                 break L;
280 |             case 10: c=s.charAt(1);
281 |                 if (c=='m') { X="implements";id=Id_implements; }
282 |                 else if (c=='n') { X="instanceof";id=Id_instanceof; }
283 |                 break L;
284 |             case 12: X="synchronized";id=Id_synchronized; break L;
285 |             }
286 |             if (X!=null && X!=s && !X.equals(s)) id = 0;
287 |         }
288 | // #/generated#
289 | // #/string_id_map#
290 |         if (id == 0) { return Token.EOF; }
291 |         return id & 0xff;
292 |     }

===== M0347 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 294-302
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
294 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
295 |       Node ret = transform(n);
296 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
297 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
298 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
299 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
300 |       }
301 |       return ret;
302 |     }

===== M0352 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
165 |     @Override
166 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
167 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
168 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
169 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
170 |           return;
171 |         }
172 | 
173 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
174 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
175 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
176 |         if (var != null) {
177 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
178 |         }
179 | 
180 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
181 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
182 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
183 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
184 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
185 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
186 |           if (assertOnChange) {
187 |             String name = n.getString();
188 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
189 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
190 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
191 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
192 |           }
193 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
194 |         }
195 |       }
196 |     }

     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties

Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {

460 |

463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 |

===== M0355 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseRoots(List<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 260-282
Documentation:

Method code:
260 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
261 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
262 |       return;
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     try {
266 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
267 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
268 | 
269 |       sourceName = "";
270 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
271 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
272 | 
273 |       for (Node root : roots) {
274 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
275 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
276 |       }
277 | 
278 |       popScope();
279 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
280 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
281 |     }
282 |   }

===== M0367 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtVarNode(Name)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 660-693
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name
   * that occurs at a VAR node. See comment for
   * {@link #updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration}.
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a")
   */
Method code:
660 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtVarNode(Name n) {
661 |     Ref ref = n.declaration;
662 |     String name = ref.node.getString();
663 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getFirstChild();
664 |     Node varNode = ref.node.getParent();
665 |     Node gramps = varNode.getParent();
666 | 
667 |     boolean isObjLit = rvalue.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT;
668 |     int numChanges = 0;
669 | 
670 |     if (isObjLit) {
671 |       numChanges += declareVarsForObjLitValues(
672 |           n, name, rvalue, varNode, gramps.getChildBefore(varNode),
673 |           gramps);
674 |     }
675 | 
676 |     numChanges += addStubsForUndeclaredProperties(n, name, gramps, varNode);
677 | 
678 |     if (isObjLit && n.canEliminate()) {
679 |       varNode.removeChild(ref.node);
680 |       if (!varNode.hasChildren()) {
681 |         gramps.removeChild(varNode);
682 |       }
683 |       numChanges++;
684 | 
685 |       // Clear out the object reference, since we've eliminated it from the
686 |       // parse tree.
687 |       ref.node = null;
688 |     }
689 | 
690 |     if (numChanges > 0) {
691 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
692 |     }
693 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.

===== M0385 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.getQualifiedName()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1726-1740
Documentation:
/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1726 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1727 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1728 |       return getString();
1729 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1730 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1731 |       if (left == null) {
1732 |         return null;
1733 |       }
1734 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1735 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1736 |       return "this";
1737 |     } else {
1738 |       return null;
1739 |     }
1740 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args

107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.

427 |   @Override
428 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
429 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
430 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.

===== M0413 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getOrCreateName(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 726-741
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets a {@link Name} instance for a global name. Creates it if necessary,
     * as well as instances for any of its prefixes that are not yet defined.
     *
     * @param name A global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c.d")
     * @return The {@link Name} instance for {@code name}
     */
Method code:
726 |     Name getOrCreateName(String name) {
727 |       Name node = nameMap.get(name);
728 |       if (node == null) {
729 |         int i = name.lastIndexOf('.');
730 |         if (i >= 0) {
731 |           String parentName = name.substring(0, i);
732 |           Name parent = getOrCreateName(parentName);
733 |           node = parent.addProperty(name.substring(i + 1), inExterns);
734 |         } else {
735 |           node = new Name(name, null, inExterns);
736 |           globalNames.add(node);

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)

/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 539-557
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
539 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
540 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
541 |         next = c.getNext();
542 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
543 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
544 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
545 |           }
546 | 
547 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
548 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
549 |             c.removeChild(name);
550 |             Node newVar = new Node(
551 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
552 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
553 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
554 |           }
555 |         }
556 |       }
557 |     }

===== M0439 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 225-274
Documentation:

Method code:
225 |     @Override
226 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
227 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
228 |         String name = n.getString();
229 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
230 |           return;
231 |         }
232 | 
233 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
234 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
235 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
236 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
237 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
238 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
239 |             expectedConst = true;
240 |           } else {
241 |             expectedConst = false;
242 | 
243 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
244 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
245 |             if (var != null) {
246 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
247 |             }
248 | 
249 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
250 |               expectedConst = true;
251 |             } else {
252 |               expectedConst = false;
253 |             }
254 |           }
255 | 
256 |           if (expectedConst) {
257 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
258 |                 "The name " + name + " is not annotated as constant.");
259 |           } else {
260 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
261 |                 "The name " + name + " should not be annotated as constant.");
262 |           }
263 |         }
264 | 
265 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
266 |         if (value == null) {
267 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
268 |         } else {
269 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
270 |               "The name " + name + " is not consistently annotated as " +
271 |               "constant.");
272 |         }
273 |       }
274 |     }

===== M0446 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 483-529
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
483 |     private void extractForInitializer(
484 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
485 | 
486 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
487 |         next = c.getNext();
488 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
489 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
490 |         switch (c.getType()) {
491 |           case Token.LABEL:
492 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
493 |             break;
494 |           case Token.FOR:
495 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
496 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
497 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
498 |                 // Transform:
499 |                 //    for (var a in b) {}
500 |                 // to:
501 |                 //    var a; for (a in b) {};
502 |                 Node newStatement = first.cloneTree();
503 |                 Node name = first.removeFirstChild();
504 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
505 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
506 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
507 |               }
508 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
509 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
510 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
511 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
512 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
513 | 
514 |               Node newStatement;
515 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
516 |               // but are handled differently.
517 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
518 |                 newStatement = init;
519 |               } else {
520 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
521 |               }
522 | 
523 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
524 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
525 |             }
526 |             break;
527 |         }
528 |       }
529 |     }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();

===== M0453 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.isGlobalVarReference(String,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 191-197
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a variable name reference in a particular scope is a
   * global variable reference.
   *
   * @param name A variable name (e.g. "a")
   * @param s The scope in which the name is referenced
   * @return Whether the name reference is a global variable reference
   */
Method code:
191 |   private boolean isGlobalVarReference(String name, Scope s) {
192 |     Scope.Var v = s.getVar(name);
193 |     if (v == null && externsScope != null) {
194 |       v = externsScope.getVar(name);
195 |     }
196 |     return v != null && !v.isLocal();
197 |   }

===== M0455 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0459 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 821-848
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
821 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
822 |     // unknown
823 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
824 |       return true;
825 |     }
826 |     // equality
827 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
828 |       return true;
829 |     }
830 |     // all type
831 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
832 |       return true;
833 |     }
834 |     // unions
835 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
836 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
837 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
838 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
839 |           return true;
840 |         }
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 |     // named types
844 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
845 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
846 |     }
847 |     return false;
848 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.has(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2117-2135
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the predicate is true for the node or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2117 |   static boolean has(Node node,
2118 |                      Predicate<Node> pred,
2119 |                      Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2120 |     if (pred.apply(node)) {
2121 |       return true;
2122 |     }
2123 | 
2124 |     if (!traverseChildrenPred.apply(node)) {
2125 |       return false;
2126 |     }
2127 | 
2128 |     for (Node c = node.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2129 |       if (has(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred)) {
2130 |         return true;
2131 |       }
2132 |     }
2133 | 
2134 |     return false;
2135 |   }

===== M0473 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

631 |     Node processObjectLiteral(ObjectLiteral literalNode) {

===== M0481 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1595-1632
Documentation:
/** Returns true if this node is equivalent semantically to another */
Method code:
1595 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node) {
1596 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1597 |       try {
1598 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1599 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1600 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1601 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1602 |             return false;
1603 |           }
1604 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1607 |           return false;
1608 |         } else {
1609 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1610 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1611 |               return false;
1612 |             }
1613 |           }
1614 |         }
1615 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1616 |         return false;
1617 |       }
1618 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1619 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1620 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1621 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1622 |         return false;
1623 |       }
1624 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1625 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1626 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1627 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1628 |         return false;
1629 |       }
1630 |     }
1631 |     return true;
1632 |   }

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

===== M0483 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.declareVarsForObjLitValues(Name,String,Node,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 722-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Declares global variables to serve as aliases for the values in an object
   * literal, optionally removing all of the object literal's keys and values.
   *
   * @param alias The object literal's flattened name (e.g. "a$b$c")
   * @param objlit The OBJLIT node
   * @param varNode The VAR node to which new global variables should be added
   *     as children
   * @param nameToAddAfter The child of {@code varNode} after which new
   *     variables should be added (may be null)
   * @param varParent {@code varNode}'s parent
   * @return The number of variables added
   */
Method code:
722 |   private int declareVarsForObjLitValues(
723 |       Name objlitName, String alias, Node objlit, Node varNode,
724 |       Node nameToAddAfter, Node varParent) {
725 |     int numVars = 0;
726 |     int arbitraryNameCounter = 0;
727 |     boolean discardKeys = !objlitName.shouldKeepKeys();
728 | 
729 |     for (Node key = objlit.getFirstChild(), nextKey; key != null;
730 |          key = nextKey) {
731 |       Node value = key.getFirstChild();
732 |       nextKey = key.getNext();
733 | 
734 |       // We generate arbitrary names for keys that aren't valid JavaScript
735 |       // identifiers, since those keys are never referenced. (If they were,
736 |       // this object literal's child names wouldn't be collapsible.) The only
737 |       // reason that we don't eliminate them entirely is the off chance that
738 |       // their values are expressions that have side effects.
739 |       boolean isJsIdentifier = key.getType() != Token.NUMBER &&
740 |                                TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key.getString());
741 |       String propName = isJsIdentifier ?
742 |           key.getString() : String.valueOf(++arbitraryNameCounter);
743 |       String propAlias = appendPropForAlias(alias, propName);
744 |       String qName = objlitName.fullName() + '.' + propName;
745 | 
746 |       Node refNode = null;
747 |       if (discardKeys) {
748 |         objlit.removeChild(key);
749 |         value.detachFromParent();
750 |       } else {
751 |         // Substitute a reference for the value.
752 |         refNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
753 |         if (key.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
754 |           refNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
755 |         }
756 | 
757 |         key.replaceChild(value, refNode);
758 |       }
759 | 
760 |       // Declare the collapsed name as a variable with the original value.
761 |       Node nameNode = Node.newString(Token.NAME, propAlias);
762 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(value);
763 |       if (key.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME)) {
764 |         nameNode.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
765 |       }
766 |       Node newVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode)
767 |           .copyInformationFromForTree(key);
768 |       if (nameToAddAfter != null) {
769 |         varParent.addChildAfter(newVar, nameToAddAfter);
770 |       } else {
771 |         varParent.addChildBefore(newVar, varNode);
772 |       }
773 |       compiler.reportCodeChange();
774 |       nameToAddAfter = newVar;
775 | 
776 |       if (isJsIdentifier) {
777 |         // Update the global name's node ancestry if it hasn't already been
778 |         // done. (Duplicate keys in an object literal can bring us here twice
779 |         // for the same global name.)
780 |         Name p = nameMap.get(qName);
781 |         if (p != null) {
782 |           if (!discardKeys) {
783 |             Ref newAlias =
784 |                 p.declaration.cloneAndReclassify(Ref.Type.ALIASING_GET);
785 |             newAlias.node = refNode;
786 |             p.addRef(newAlias);
787 |           }
788 | 
789 |           p.declaration.node = nameNode;
790 | 
791 |           if (value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
792 |             checkForHosedThisReferences(value, value.getJSDocInfo(), p);
793 |           }
794 |         }
795 |       }
796 | 
797 |       numVars++;
798 |     }
799 |     return numVars;
800 |   }

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
151 |           }
152 |           n.setString(newName);
153 |           t.getCompiler().reportCodeChange();
154 |         }
155 |         break;
156 | 
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // Remove function recursive name (if any).
159 |         nameStack.pop();
160 |         break;
161 | 
162 |       case Token.CATCH:
163 |         // Remove catch except name from the stack of names.
164 |         nameStack.pop();
165 |         break;
166 |     }

===== M0505 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 75-655
Documentation:

Method code:
75 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
76 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
77 |       return;
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     int type = n.getType();
81 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
82 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
83 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
84 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
85 | 
86 |     // Handle all binary operators
87 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
88 |       Preconditions.checkState(
89 |           childCount == 2,
90 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
91 |           opstr, childCount);
92 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
93 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
94 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
95 | 
96 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
97 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
98 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
99 | 
100 |       // Handle associativity.
101 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
102 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
103 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
104 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
105 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
106 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
107 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
108 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
109 |       } else {
110 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
111 |       }
112 |       return;
113 |     }
114 | 
115 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
116 | 
117 |     switch (type) {
118 |       case Token.TRY: {
119 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
120 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
121 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
122 | 
123 |         add("try");
124 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
125 | 
126 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
127 |         // isn't a catch block
128 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
129 |         if (catchblock != null) {
130 |           add(catchblock);
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         if (childCount == 3) {
134 |           add("finally");
135 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
136 |         }
137 |         break;
138 |       }
139 | 
140 |       case Token.CATCH:
141 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
142 |         add("catch(");
143 |         add(first);
144 |         add(")");
145 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
146 |         break;
147 | 
148 |       case Token.THROW:
149 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
150 |         add("throw");
151 |         add(first);
152 | 
153 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
154 |         // parse this.
155 |         cc.endStatement(true);
156 |         break;
157 | 
158 |       case Token.RETURN:
159 |         add("return");
160 |         if (childCount == 1) {
161 |           add(first);
162 |         } else {
163 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
164 |         }
165 |         cc.endStatement();
166 |         break;
167 | 
168 |       case Token.VAR:
169 |         if (first != null) {
170 |           add("var ");
171 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
172 |         }
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
176 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
177 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.NAME:
181 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
182 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
183 |         } else {
184 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
185 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
186 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
187 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
188 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
189 |           } else {
190 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
191 |             // precedence.
192 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
193 |           }
194 |         }
195 |         break;
196 | 
197 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
198 |         add("[");
199 |         addList(first, (int[]) n.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP));
200 |         add("]");
201 |         break;
202 | 
203 |       case Token.LP:
204 |         add("(");
205 |         addList(first);
206 |         add(")");
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.COMMA:
210 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
211 |         addList(first, false, context);
212 |         break;
213 | 
214 |       case Token.NUMBER:
215 |         Preconditions.checkState(
216 |             childCount ==
217 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
218 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
219 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
220 |         break;
221 | 
222 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
223 |       case Token.VOID:
224 |       case Token.NOT:
225 |       case Token.BITNOT:
226 |       case Token.POS:
227 |       case Token.NEG: {
228 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
229 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
230 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
231 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
232 |         break;
233 |       }
234 | 
235 |       case Token.HOOK: {
236 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
237 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
238 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
239 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
240 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
241 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
242 |         addExpr(last, 1);
243 |         break;
244 |       }
245 | 
246 |       case Token.REGEXP:
247 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
248 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
249 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
250 |         }
251 | 
252 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
253 | 
254 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
255 |         if (childCount == 2) {
256 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
257 |         } else {
258 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
259 |           add(regexp);
260 |         }
261 |         break;
262 | 
263 |       case Token.GET_REF:
264 |         add(first);
265 |         break;
266 | 
267 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
268 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
269 |         add(first);
270 |         add(".");
271 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
272 |         break;
273 | 
274 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
275 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
276 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
277 |         }
278 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
279 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
280 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
281 |           add("(");
282 |         }
283 | 
284 |         add("function");
285 |         add(first);
286 | 
287 |         add(first.getNext());
288 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
289 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
290 | 
291 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
292 |           add(")");
293 |         }
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
297 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
298 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
299 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
300 |         }
301 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
302 |         if (preserveBlock) {
303 |           cc.beginBlock();
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
307 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
308 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
309 |                 !preserveBlock &&
310 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
311 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
312 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
313 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
314 | 
315 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
316 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
317 |             cc.endStatement();
318 |           }
319 | 
320 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
321 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
322 |           }
323 | 
324 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
325 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
326 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
327 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
328 |           }
329 |         }
330 |         if (preserveBlock) {
331 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
332 |         }
333 |         break;
334 |       }
335 | 
336 |       case Token.FOR:
337 |         if (childCount == 4) {
338 |           add("for(");
339 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
340 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
341 |           } else {
342 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
343 |           }
344 |           add(";");
345 |           add(first.getNext());
346 |           add(";");
347 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
348 |           add(")");
349 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
350 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
351 |         } else {
352 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
353 |           add("for(");
354 |           add(first);
355 |           add("in");
356 |           add(first.getNext());
357 |           add(")");
358 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
359 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
360 |         }
361 |         break;
362 | 
363 |       case Token.DO:
364 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
365 |         add("do");
366 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
367 |         add("while(");
368 |         add(last);
369 |         add(")");
370 |         cc.endStatement();
371 |         break;
372 | 
373 |       case Token.WHILE:
374 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
375 |         add("while(");
376 |         add(first);
377 |         add(")");
378 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
379 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
380 |         break;
381 | 
382 |       case Token.EMPTY:
383 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
384 |         break;
385 | 
386 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
387 |         Preconditions.checkState(
388 |             childCount == 2,
389 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
390 |         Preconditions.checkState(
391 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
392 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
393 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
394 |         if (needsParens) {
395 |           add("(");
396 |         }
397 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
398 |         if (needsParens) {
399 |           add(")");
400 |         }
401 |         add(".");
402 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
403 |         break;
404 |       }
405 | 
406 |       case Token.GETELEM:
407 |         Preconditions.checkState(
408 |             childCount == 2,
409 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
410 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
411 |         add("[");
412 |         add(first.getNext());
413 |         add("]");
414 |         break;
415 | 
416 |       case Token.WITH:
417 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
418 |         add("with(");
419 |         add(first);
420 |         add(")");
421 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
422 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
423 |         break;
424 | 
425 |       case Token.INC:
426 |       case Token.DEC: {
427 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
428 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
429 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
430 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
431 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
432 |         if (postProp != 0) {
433 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
434 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
435 |         } else {
436 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
437 |           add(first);
438 |         }
439 |         break;
440 |       }
441 | 
442 |       case Token.CALL:
443 |         // We have two special cases here:
444 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
445 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
446 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
447 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
448 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
449 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
450 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
451 |         // that must be preserved.
452 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
453 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
454 |           add("(0,");
455 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
456 |           add(")");
457 |         } else {
458 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
459 |         }
460 |         add("(");
461 |         addList(first.getNext());
462 |         add(")");
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       case Token.IF:
466 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
467 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
468 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
469 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
470 |           cc.beginBlock();
471 |         }
472 | 
473 |         add("if(");
474 |         add(first);
475 |         add(")");
476 | 
477 |         if (hasElse) {
478 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
479 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
480 |           add("else");
481 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
482 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
483 |         } else {
484 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
485 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
486 |         }
487 | 
488 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
489 |           cc.endBlock();
490 |         }
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       case Token.NULL:
494 |       case Token.THIS:
495 |       case Token.FALSE:
496 |       case Token.TRUE:
497 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
498 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
499 |         break;
500 | 
501 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
502 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
503 |         add("continue");
504 |         if (childCount == 1) {
505 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
506 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
507 |           }
508 |           add(" ");
509 |           add(first);
510 |         }
511 |         cc.endStatement();
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
515 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
516 |         add("debugger");
517 |         cc.endStatement();
518 |         break;
519 | 
520 |       case Token.BREAK:
521 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
522 |         add("break");
523 |         if (childCount == 1) {
524 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
525 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
526 |           }
527 |           add(" ");
528 |           add(first);
529 |         }
530 |         cc.endStatement();
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
534 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
535 | 
536 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
537 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
538 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
539 |         cc.endStatement();
540 |         break;
541 | 
542 |       case Token.NEW:
543 |         add("new ");
544 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
545 | 
546 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
547 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
548 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
549 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
550 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
551 |         }
552 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
553 | 
554 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
555 |         Node next = first.getNext();
556 |         if (next != null) {
557 |           add("(");
558 |           addList(next);
559 |           add(")");
560 |         }
561 |         break;
562 | 
563 |       case Token.STRING:
564 |         if (childCount !=
565 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
566 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
567 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
568 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
569 |         }
570 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.DELPROP:
574 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
575 |         add("delete ");
576 |         add(first);
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
580 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
581 |         if (needsParens) {
582 |           add("(");
583 |         }
584 |         add("{");
585 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
586 |           if (c != first) {
587 |             cc.listSeparator();
588 |           }
589 | 
590 |           // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
591 |           // not JavaScript keywords
592 |           if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
593 |               !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
594 |               TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
595 |               // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
596 |               // unicode escaped.
597 |               NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
598 |             add(c.getString());
599 |           } else {
600 |             addExpr(c, 1);
601 |           }
602 |           add(":");
603 |           addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
604 |         }
605 |         add("}");
606 |         if (needsParens) {
607 |           add(")");
608 |         }
609 |         break;
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       case Token.SWITCH:
613 |         add("switch(");
614 |         add(first);
615 |         add(")");
616 |         cc.beginBlock();
617 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
618 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
619 |         break;
620 | 
621 |       case Token.CASE:
622 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
623 |         add("case ");
624 |         add(first);
625 |         addCaseBody(last);
626 |         break;
627 | 
628 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
629 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
630 |         add("default");
631 |         addCaseBody(first);
632 |         break;
633 | 
634 |       case Token.LABEL:
635 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
636 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
637 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
638 |         }
639 |         add(first);
640 |         add(":");
641 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
642 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
643 |         break;
644 | 
645 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
646 |       // ignored for our purposes.
647 |       case Token.SETNAME:
648 |         break;
649 | 
650 |       default:
651 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
652 |     }
653 | 
654 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
655 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 666-704
Documentation:
/**
     * Updates our respresentation of the global namespace to reflect a read
     * of a global name's longest prefix before the "prototype" property if the
     * name includes the "prototype" property. Does nothing otherwise.
     *
     * @param t The current node traversal
     * @param n The node currently being visited
     * @param parent {@code n}'s parent
     * @param name The global name (e.g. "a" or "a.b.c.d")
     * @return Whether the name was handled
     */
Method code:
666 |     boolean maybeHandlePrototypePrefix(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent,
667 |         String name) {
668 |       // We use a string-based approach instead of inspecting the parse tree
669 |       // to avoid complexities with object literals, possibly nested, beneath
670 |       // assignments.
671 | 
672 |       int numLevelsToRemove;
673 |       String prefix;
674 |       if (name.endsWith(".prototype")) {
675 |         numLevelsToRemove = 1;
676 |         prefix = name.substring(0, name.length() - 10);
677 |       } else {
678 |         int i = name.indexOf(".prototype.");
679 |         if (i == -1) {
680 |           return false;
681 |         }
682 |         prefix = name.substring(0, i);
683 |         numLevelsToRemove = 2;
684 |         i = name.indexOf('.', i + 11);
685 |         while (i >= 0) {
686 |           numLevelsToRemove++;
687 |           i = name.indexOf('.', i + 1);
688 |         }
689 |       }
690 | 
691 |       if (parent != null && NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
692 |         // Object literal keys have no prefix that's referenced directly per
693 |         // key, so we're done.
694 |         return true;
695 |       }
696 | 
697 |       for (int i = 0; i < numLevelsToRemove; i++) {
698 |         parent = n;
699 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
700 |       }
701 | 
702 |       handleGet(t, n, parent, prefix, Ref.Type.PROTOTYPE_GET);
703 |       return true;
704 |     }

===== M0510 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 400-416
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
400 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
401 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
402 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
403 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
404 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
405 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
406 |         String inputName = input.getName();
407 | 
408 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
409 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
410 |         // will get an error down the line.
411 |         inputs.add(input);
412 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
413 |       }
414 |     }
415 |     return inputs;
416 |   }

===== M0518 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0520 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 563-595
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
563 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
564 |       Preconditions.checkState(
565 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
566 |       Node previous = null;
567 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
568 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
569 |       // are already in the right place.
570 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
571 |         previous = current;
572 |         current = current.getNext();
573 |       }
574 | 
575 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
576 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
577 |       while (current != null) {
578 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
579 |         Node next = current.getNext();
580 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
581 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
582 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
583 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
584 | 
585 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
586 |           // previous declarations).
587 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
588 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
589 |         } else {
590 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
591 |           previous = current;
592 |         }
593 |         current = next;
594 |       }
595 |     }

===== M0522 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.BuildGlobalNamespace.getValueType(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 402-424
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the type of a value or simple expression.
     *
     * @param n An rvalue in an assignment or variable declaration (not null)
     * @return A {@link Name.Type}
     */
Method code:
402 |     Name.Type getValueType(Node n) {
403 |       switch (n.getType()) {
404 |         case Token.OBJECTLIT:
405 |           return Name.Type.OBJECTLIT;
406 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
407 |           return Name.Type.FUNCTION;
408 |         case Token.OR:
409 |           // Recurse on the second value. If the first value were an object
410 |           // literal or function, then the OR would be meaningless and the
411 |           // second value would be dead code. Assume that if the second value
412 |           // is an object literal or function, then the first value will also
413 |           // evaluate to one when it doesn't evaluate to false.
414 |           return getValueType(n.getLastChild());
415 |         case Token.HOOK:
416 |           // The same line of reasoning used for the OR case applies here.
417 |           Node second = n.getFirstChild().getNext();
418 |           Name.Type t = getValueType(second);
419 |           if (t != Name.Type.OTHER) return t;
420 |           Node third = second.getNext();
421 |           return getValueType(third);
422 |       }
423 |       return Name.Type.OTHER;
424 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1404-1427
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1404 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1405 |     boolean eq = false;
1406 | 
1407 |     if (type == node2.getType() && getChildCount() == node2.getChildCount()
1408 |         && getNodeClass(this) == getNodeClass(node2)) {
1409 |       eq = this.isEquivalentTo(node2);
1410 |     }
1411 | 
1412 |     if (!eq) {
1413 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1414 |     }
1415 | 
1416 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1417 |     Node n, n2;
1418 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1419 |          res == null && n != null;
1420 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1421 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1422 |       if (res != null) {
1423 |         return res;
1424 |       }
1425 |     }
1426 |     return res;
1427 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.flattenNameRefAtDepth(String,Node,int,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 389-406
Documentation:
/**
   * Flattens a particular prefix of a single name reference.
   *
   * @param alias A flattened prefix name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param n The node corresponding to a subproperty name (e.g. "a.b.c.d")
   * @param depth The difference in depth between the property name and
   *    the prefix name (e.g. 2)
   * @param originalName String version of the property name.
   */
Method code:
389 |   private void flattenNameRefAtDepth(String alias, Node n, int depth,
390 |       String originalName) {
391 |     // This method has to work for both GETPROP chains and, in rare cases,
392 |     // OBJLIT keys, possibly nested. That's why we check for children before
393 |     // proceeding. In the OBJLIT case, we don't need to do anything.
394 |     int nType = n.getType();
395 |     boolean isQName = nType == Token.NAME || nType == Token.GETPROP;
396 |     boolean isObjKey = nType == Token.STRING || nType == Token.NUMBER;
397 |     Preconditions.checkState(isObjKey || isQName);
398 |     if (isQName) {
399 |       for (int i = 1; i < depth && n.hasChildren(); i++) {
400 |         n = n.getFirstChild();
401 |       }
402 |       if (n.hasChildren()) {
403 |         flattenNameRef(alias, n.getFirstChild(), n, originalName);
404 |       }
405 |     }
406 |   }

===== M0530 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateSimpleDeclaration(String,Name,Ref)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 477-540
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the initial assignment to a collapsible property at global scope
   * by changing it to a variable declaration (e.g. a.b = 1 -> var a$b = 1).
   * The property's value may either be a primitive or an object literal or
   * function whose properties aren't collapsible.
   *
   * @param alias The flattened property name (e.g. "a$b")
   * @param refName The name for the reference being updated.
   * @param ref An object containing information about the assignment getting
   *     updated
   */
Method code:
477 |   private void updateSimpleDeclaration(String alias, Name refName, Ref ref) {
478 |     Node rvalue = ref.node.getNext();
479 |     Node parent = ref.node.getParent();
480 |     Node gramps = parent.getParent();
481 |     Node greatGramps = gramps.getParent();
482 |     Node greatGreatGramps = greatGramps.getParent();
483 | 
484 | 
485 |     // Create the new alias node.
486 |     Node nameNode = NodeUtil.newName(
487 |         compiler.getCodingConvention(), alias, gramps.getFirstChild(),
488 |         refName.fullName());
489 |     NodeUtil.copyNameAnnotations(ref.node.getLastChild(), nameNode);
490 | 
491 |     if (gramps.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
492 |       // BEFORE: a.b.c = ...;
493 |       //   exprstmt
494 |       //     assign
495 |       //       getprop
496 |       //         getprop
497 |       //           name a
498 |       //           string b
499 |       //         string c
500 |       //       NODE
501 |       // AFTER: var a$b$c = ...;
502 |       //   var
503 |       //     name a$b$c
504 |       //       NODE
505 | 
506 |       // Remove the rvalue (NODE).
507 |       parent.removeChild(rvalue);
508 |       nameNode.addChildToFront(rvalue);
509 | 
510 |       Node varNode = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode);
511 |       greatGramps.replaceChild(gramps, varNode);
512 |     } else {
513 |       // This must be a complex assignment.
514 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(ref.getTwin());
515 | 
516 |       // BEFORE:
517 |       // ... (x.y = 3);
518 |       //
519 |       // AFTER:
520 |       // var x$y;
521 |       // ... (x$y = 3);
522 | 
523 |       Node current = gramps;
524 |       Node currentParent = gramps.getParent();
525 |       for (; currentParent.getType() != Token.SCRIPT &&
526 |              currentParent.getType() != Token.BLOCK;
527 |            current = currentParent,
528 |            currentParent = currentParent.getParent()) {}
529 | 
530 |       // Create a stub variable declaration right
531 |       // before the current statement.
532 |       Node stubVar = new Node(Token.VAR, nameNode.cloneTree())
533 |           .copyInformationFrom(nameNode);
534 |       currentParent.addChildBefore(stubVar, current);
535 | 
536 |       parent.replaceChild(ref.node, nameNode);
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     compiler.reportCodeChange();
540 |   }

===== M0550 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 408-452
Documentation:

Method code:
408 |   @Override
409 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
410 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
411 |       return true;
412 |     }
413 | 
414 |     // Union types
415 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
416 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
417 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
418 |       return false;
419 |     }
420 | 
421 |     // record types
422 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
423 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Interfaces
427 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
428 |     // to the interface instance.
429 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
430 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
431 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
432 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
433 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
434 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
435 |           return true;
436 |         }
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // other prototype based objects
441 |     if (that != null) {
442 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
443 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
444 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
445 |         // to avoid guessing.
446 |         return true;
447 |       }
448 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
449 |     }
450 | 
451 |     return false;
452 |   }
