===== M0554 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

/**

591 |   private static String toSource(Node root, Format outputFormat,
592 |                                  boolean lineBreak,  int lineLengthThreshold,

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 425-445
Documentation:

Method code:
425 |     @Override
426 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
427 |       if (lineBreak) {
428 |         if (sawFunction) {
429 |           startNewLine();
430 |           sawFunction = false;
431 |         }
432 |       }
433 | 
434 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
435 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
436 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
437 |       int len = code.length();
438 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
439 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
440 |         if (ch == ';') {
441 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
442 |         }
443 |       }
444 |       maybeCutLine();
445 |     }

===== M0600 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 340-366
Documentation:
/**
     * Mark names and properties that are constants by convention.
     */
Method code:
340 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
341 |       Preconditions.checkState(
342 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME || n.getType() == Token.STRING);
343 | 
344 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
345 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
346 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
347 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
348 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
349 |           (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
350 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
351 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME || isProperty) {
352 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
353 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
354 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
355 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
356 |           if (assertOnChange) {
357 |             String name = n.getString();
358 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
359 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
360 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
361 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
362 |           }
363 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
364 |         }
365 |       }
366 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 250-293

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
250 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
251 |     this.type = type;
252 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
253 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
254 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
255 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
256 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
257 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
258 |       int i = 0;
259 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
260 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
261 |         i++;
262 |       }
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
266 | 
267 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
268 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
269 |       return;
270 |     }
271 | 
272 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
273 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
274 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
275 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
276 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
277 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
278 |       trace.truncateEvents();
279 |     }
280 | 
281 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
282 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
283 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
284 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
285 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
286 |                     + "try/finally block. "
287 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
288 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
289 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
290 |     }
291 | 
292 |     trace.startEvent(this);
293 |   }

===== M0617 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(Name,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 552-564
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the first initialization (a.k.a "declaration") of a global name.
   * This involves flattening the global name (if it's not just a global
   * variable name already), collapsing object literal keys into global
   * variables, declaring stub global variables for properties added later
   * in a local scope, and eliminating the global name entirely (if possible).
   *
   * @param n An object representing a global name (e.g. "a", "a.b.c")
   * @param alias The flattened name for {@code n} (e.g. "a", "a$b$c")
   */
Method code:
552 |   private void updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclaration(Name n, String alias) {
553 |     switch (n.declaration.node.getParent().getType()) {
554 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
555 |         updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtAssignNode(n, alias);
556 |         break;
557 |       case Token.VAR:
558 |         updateObjLitOrFunctionDeclarationAtVarNode(n);
559 |         break;
560 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
561 |         updateFunctionDeclarationAtFunctionNode(n);
562 |         break;
563 |     }
564 |   }

===== M0621 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseProperties.inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseProperties.java
Original range: 158-189
Documentation:
/**
   * For each qualified name N in the global scope, we check if:
   * (a) No ancestor of N is ever aliased or assigned an unknown value type.
   *     (If N = "a.b.c", "a" and "a.b" are never aliased).
   * (b) N has exactly one write, and it lives in the global scope.
   * (c) N is aliased in a local scope.
   *
   * If (a) is true, then GlobalNamespace must know all the writes to N.
   * If (a) and (b) are true, then N cannot change during the execution of
   *    a local scope.
   * If (a) and (b) and (c) are true, then the alias can be inlined if the
   *    alias obeys the usual rules for how we decide whether a variable is
   *    inlineable.
   * @see InlineVariables
   */
Method code:
158 |   private void inlineAliases(GlobalNamespace namespace) {
159 |     // Invariant: All the names in the worklist meet condition (a).
160 |     Deque<Name> workList = new ArrayDeque<Name>(namespace.getNameForest());
161 |     while (!workList.isEmpty()) {
162 |       Name name = workList.pop();
163 | 
164 |       if (name.globalSets == 1 && name.localSets == 0 &&
165 |           name.aliasingGets > 0) {
166 |         // {@code name} meets condition (b). Find all of its local aliases
167 |         // and try to inline them.
168 |         List<Ref> refs = Lists.newArrayList(name.refs);
169 |         for (Ref ref : refs) {
170 |           if (ref.type == Type.ALIASING_GET && ref.scope.isLocal()) {
171 |             // {@code name} meets condition (c). Try to inline it.
172 |             if (inlineAliasIfPossible(ref, namespace)) {
173 |               name.removeRef(ref);
174 |             }
175 |           }
176 |         }
177 |       }
178 | 
179 |       // Check if {@code name} has any aliases left after the
180 |       // local-alias-inlining above.
181 |       if ((name.type == Name.Type.OBJECTLIT ||
182 |            name.type == Name.Type.FUNCTION) &&
183 |           name.aliasingGets == 0 && name.props != null) {
184 |         // All of {@code name}'s children meet condition (a), so they can be
185 |         // added to the worklist.
186 |         workList.addAll(name.props);
187 |       }
188 |     }
189 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java

/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
857 |   static int precedence(int type) {
858 |     switch (type) {
859 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
860 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
861 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
862 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
863 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
864 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
865 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
866 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
867 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
868 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
869 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
870 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
871 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
872 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
873 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
874 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
875 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
876 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
877 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
878 |       case Token.EQ:
879 |       case Token.NE:
880 |       case Token.SHEQ:
881 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
882 |       case Token.LT:
883 |       case Token.GT:
884 |       case Token.LE:
885 |       case Token.GE:
886 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
887 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
888 |       case Token.LSH:
889 |       case Token.RSH:
890 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
891 |       case Token.SUB:
892 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
893 |       case Token.MUL:
894 |       case Token.MOD:
895 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
896 |       case Token.INC:
897 |       case Token.DEC:
898 |       case Token.NEW:
899 |       case Token.DELPROP:
900 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
901 |       case Token.VOID:
902 |       case Token.NOT:
903 |       case Token.BITNOT:
904 |       case Token.POS:
905 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
906 | 
907 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:

909 |       case Token.EMPTY:
910 |       case Token.FALSE:

927 |

931 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 226-262
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
226 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
227 |     this.options = options;
228 |     if (errorManager == null) {
229 |       if (outStream == null) {
230 |         setErrorManager(
231 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
232 |       } else {
233 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
234 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
235 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
236 |         setErrorManager(printer);
237 |       }
238 |     }
239 | 
240 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
241 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
242 |     guards.add(
243 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
244 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
245 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
246 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
247 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
251 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
252 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
253 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
254 |     // error off.
255 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
256 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
257 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
258 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
260 |     }
261 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
262 |   }

55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
57 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
58 |       addGuards(Lists.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
59 |     } else {

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GlobalNamespace.Name.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GlobalNamespace.java
Original range: 896-925
Documentation:

Method code:
896 |     boolean canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames() {
897 |       if (type == Type.OTHER || globalSets != 1 || localSets != 0) {
898 |         return false;
899 |       }
900 | 
901 |       // Don't try to collapse if the one global set is a twin reference.
902 |       // We could theoretically handle this case in CollapseProperties, but
903 |       // it's probably not worth the effort.
904 |       Preconditions.checkNotNull(declaration);
905 |       if (declaration.getTwin() != null) {
906 |         return false;
907 |       }
908 | 
909 |       if (isClassOrEnum) {
910 |         return true;
911 |       }
912 | 
913 |       // If this is a key of an aliased object literal, then it will be aliased
914 |       // later. So we won't be able to collapse its properties.
915 |       if (parent != null && parent.shouldKeepKeys()) {
916 |         return false;
917 |       }
918 | 
919 |       // If this is aliased, then its properties can't be collapsed either.
920 |       if (type != Type.FUNCTION && aliasingGets > 0) {
921 |         return false;
922 |       }
923 | 
924 |       return (parent == null || parent.canCollapseUnannotatedChildNames());
925 |     }

105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.CATCH:
127 |         {
128 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
129 | 
130 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
132 | 
133 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
134 |         }
135 |         break;
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     return true;
139 |   }

Original range: 230-323
Documentation:

Method code:
230 |     @Override
231 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
232 |       if (nodeFilter != null && !nodeFilter.apply(n)) {
233 |         return;
234 |       }
235 | 
236 |       // If we are traversing the externs, then we save a pointer to the scope
237 |       // generated by them, so that we can do lookups in it later.
238 |       if (externsRoot != null && n == externsRoot) {
239 |         externsScope = t.getScope();
240 |       }
241 | 
242 |       String name;
243 |       boolean isSet = false;
244 |       Name.Type type = Name.Type.OTHER;
245 |       boolean isPropAssign = false;
246 | 
247 |       switch (n.getType()) {
248 |         case Token.STRING:
249 |           // This may be a key in an object literal declaration.
250 |           name = null;
251 |           if (parent != null && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
252 |             name = getNameForObjLitKey(n);
253 |           }
254 |           if (name == null) return;
255 |           isSet = true;
256 |           type = getValueType(n.getFirstChild());
257 |           break;
258 |         case Token.NAME:
259 |           // This may be a variable get or set.
260 |           if (parent != null) {
261 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
262 |               case Token.VAR:
263 |                 isSet = true;
264 |                 Node rvalue = n.getFirstChild();
265 |                 type = rvalue == null ? Name.Type.OTHER : getValueType(rvalue);
266 |                 break;
267 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
268 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
269 |                   isSet = true;
270 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
271 |                 }
272 |                 break;
273 |               case Token.GETPROP:
274 |                 return;
275 |               case Token.FUNCTION:
276 |                 Node gramps = parent.getParent();
277 |                 if (gramps == null ||
278 |                     NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) return;
279 |                 isSet = true;
280 |                 type = Name.Type.FUNCTION;
281 |                 break;
282 |             }
283 |           }
284 |           name = n.getString();
285 |           break;
286 |         case Token.GETPROP:
287 |           // This may be a namespaced name get or set.
288 |           if (parent != null) {
289 |             switch (parent.getType()) {
290 |               case Token.ASSIGN:
291 |                 if (parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
292 |                   isSet = true;
293 |                   type = getValueType(n.getNext());
294 |                   isPropAssign = true;
295 |                 }
296 |                 break;
297 |               case Token.GETPROP:
298 |                 return;
299 |             }
300 |           }
301 |           name = n.getQualifiedName();
302 |           if (name == null) return;
303 |           break;
304 |         default:
305 |           return;
306 |       }
307 | 
308 |       // We are only interested in global names.
309 |       Scope scope = t.getScope();
310 |       if (!isGlobalNameReference(name, scope)) {
311 |         return;
312 |       }
313 | 
314 |       if (isSet) {
315 |         if (isGlobalScope(scope)) {
316 |           handleSetFromGlobal(t, n, parent, name, isPropAssign, type);
317 |         } else {
318 |           handleSetFromLocal(t, n, parent, name);
319 |         }
320 |       } else {
321 |         handleGet(t, n, parent, name);
322 |       }
323 |     }

488 |     @Override
489 |     Node processFunctionCall(FunctionCall callNode) {
490 |       Node node = newNode(transformTokenType(callNode.getType()),
491 |                            transform(callNode.getTarget()));
492 |       for (AstNode child : callNode.getArguments()) {
493 |         node.addChildToBack(transform(child));
494 |       }
495 | 
496 |       int leftParamPos = callNode.getAbsolutePosition() + callNode.getLp();
497 |       node.setLineno(callNode.getLineno());
498 |       node.setCharno(position2charno(leftParamPos));
499 |       return node;
500 |     }
